<h2>photograph, visible, photo, lighting</h2>

<h3><a href="https://www.threads.com/@saars.foto/post/DgIOPVlGyZo">Pattern Tuesday 🔷 Patterns hide in plain sight - today&#x27;s prompt is repetition, anywhere you find it.
